rating: 5
body: The food, service, atmosphere and location are all 5 stars!  I cannot say enough about this restaurant!  Make sure you have a reservation.
My daughters and I were seated on the patio that overlooks the Gulf...a perfect location to watch the sunset. There are heaters in the table area to keep you cozy along with firepits at each end of the patio with ample comfortable seating, where you can sit after dinner to enjoy the views and ambiance.
Gabnail was our server on Saturday night. Top Notch! He made us feel very comfortable; was friendly and knowledgeable about all the food selections and wines. Miriam was also sweet and quietly friendly.  Josh is the manager and checked on us a couple times to see if we needed anything.  He definitely went the extra mile in providing excellent customer service!! All of the food was delicious. So good that we decided to go back again the next night!
As we walked in, the Hostess (I'm sorry that I forgot her name!) was warm and friendly and welcomed us back!  Natalie was our server on Sunday evening and again... exceptional! We wanted to be seated on the patio again, even though it was a little chilly that night.  Josh made sure we had 2 heaters by our table and we were quite comfortable. Again the service and food were excellent!
This restaurant has a beautiful setting and the food is exceptional. Above all, though, the staff makes your dining experience memorable!  Thank you to all!!
